# Design decisions — the register

> Kept by the design-system agent (`.claude/agents/design-system.md`), decided by the owner.
> Four lists and a log. An agent or a teammate reads this **before** proposing anything: an
> exception written here is not a finding, and a question already asked is not asked twice.
>
> The narrative of *why* the system looks the way it does stays in `docs/DESIGN-SYSTEM.md`
> and the session notes. This file is only the ledger: what is open, what was decided, what
> is allowed to differ.

---

## 1. Open — waiting for the owner

Each entry is a question that can be answered in a word. Newest first.

*O-92 (the Wash, six choices) answered Nov 21, 2026 — "yes, all six", as recommended; see §2 (plates of a wash: decided, not built — §4).* *O-54 (a Set dropped on a Compose cell) and O-20 … O-23 (colour component, picker, TuneSutra → tools, names across tools — 45 open sub-decisions) answered Nov 17, 2026 — all as recommended; see §2. Decided, not built: see §4.* *O-60, O-61, O-62 (FVS Variations, first and second level) and O-64 (Element › Divide into parts) answered Nov 17, 2026 — all as built; see §2.* *O-83, O-80 and O-79 (the words of TuneSutra Gradient + Grain, of the FVS Symbol of Oct 28 and of the Selection) answered Nov 17, 2026 — all as built; O-78 closed with them (superseded; its (d) / (f) parked with Suggest); see §2.* *O-81 and O-82 (the words of a Symbol's Figure variations and of a Symbol made from a variation) answered Nov 2, 2026 — all as the REVIEW recommended; see §2.* *O-80 (d) (a keyboard path for the Brush) answered Oct 28, 2026 — **B**, the Brush a mode in the bar; see §2.* *O-63 (the FVS steps in the left dock) answered Oct 28, 2026 — the steps have their own bar at the top, `.org-floatbar--top`; see §2.* *O-1 (keep `--hit-min`) and O-2 (tokens.json is a mirror) answered Oct 13, 2026 — see §2; O-3 rewritten to its one remaining question; O-64 (a) answered — see §2.* *O-71 (the 60° turn on hexagon / triangle lattice cells) answered Oct 13, 2026 — fixed at the source, see §2.* *O-70 (a), (g), (d), (b) and (f) answered Oct 13, 2026 — see §2; (c) obsolete (no chevron now); (e) answered too — the stroke stays centred on the outline. O-70 is closed; both rows left §1 in the Oct 13 audit DOCUMENT run.* *O-67 (the step-boards prototype's five questions) answered Oct 11, 2026 — see §2; nothing built yet.* *O-66 (FVS step boards: Gallery, undo, Clear board) answered Oct 11, 2026 — see §2.* *O-59 (Variations and Rotate & mirror on the cells) answered Oct 9, 2026 — yes, with *Rules describe, Variations explore* — see §2.* *O-40 … O-53 and N4 / N6 of O-23 answered by Diego Oct 8, 2026 — see §2; O-54 and O-55 opened from those answers.* *O-39 (promote the Graph menu) answered Oct 7, 2026 — “ok, promote it to the shared canvas” — and is in §2.* *O-30 … O-35 (the Figure graph consults — node canvas and page layout, Oct 7, 2026) answered the same day by Diego and in §2, with the rest of the Figure graph decisions (rounds 1 and 2, plan `make-a-plan-to-declarative-clover.md`). O-36 and O-37 were answered by test at the Phase 4 UX checkpoint and are in §2 (closed here Oct 7, 2026, after the Figure audit: minimap *not now*, re-evaluate past ~20 Figures — the chip view starts below **50 %** zoom, not 35 %; `FIGURE_RENDER_CAP` = 24, default Variations = 4).* *O-14 … O-18 (Oct 3, 2026) answered the same day — "ok to all" — and are in §2 (O-17 also in §3).* *O-13 (the sign-in animation), opened and answered Oct 2, 2026 — see §2.* *Diego, Oct 2, 2026: O-1 … O-5 noted, to be decided later — do not act on the recommendations until he answers. (O-6 … O-10, the templates, were answered the same day — "sì a tutte e cinque" — and are in §2.)*

## 2. Decisions

Newest first. One line each; the reason and the place it is enforced are what make it stick.

| Date | Decision | Reason | Enforced by |
|---|---|---|---|
| 2026-11-21 | **Wash — the six O-92 choices confirmed** (Diego, Nov 21, 2026: *yes, all six*). (a) The Type is named **Wash** (model key `field` unchanged). (b) The row **Wash over** *Each shape · All the shapes*, default **Each shape**. (c) **Plates of a wash: one plate per stop**, the bands halftoned onto the stops' plates — not a plate per band (decided, not built — §4; until then Plates export a plate per band). (d) A palette's **house Shading may be a Wash**, and TuneSutra's Colour mode **Preview stays drawn by the shared Wash** (`fieldValue`). (e) In a tool with **no sheet** the row stays hidden and a wash saved *All the shapes* draws *Each shape*, keeping `span: 'sheet'` — silently. (f) The row is **Ripple** (key `wave`). The provisional mark on Wash, Slope, Ripple, Wash over, Each shape, All the shapes is lifted. | One fill, one word, one maths from the preview to the export; one ink per film, as O-90 h | `shared/shade-editor.js` (`FIELD_RANGES`, the Wash over row), `shared/shading.js` (`fieldValue`, `fieldPlates`); `scripts/test-shading.mjs`, `test-shading-field.mjs`, `test-wash-tools.mjs` |
| 2026-11-21 | **Wash — a fourth gradient Type in the shared shade editor; every visible name provisional** (Diego, Nov 21, 2026, on TuneSutra's Colour mode preview: *lo trovo bellissimo*, then *quello che vediamo sia uguale anche in SVG export*; design-system CONSULT the same day). Type **Flat · Linear · Radial · Wash**; with Wash, after Angle: **Size** (aria *Wash size*, N6) · **Slope** % · **Ripple** % (key `wave`; *Wave* is Trellis's tracks' row — O-92 f, applied after the REVIEW) · **Seed** (aria *Wash seed*, N4) · sub-label **Wash over** *Each shape · All the shapes*, shown only where the tool gives a sheet (`ctx.sheet`: Trellis through `paletteKit.strip({ sheet })`, Radial + Pulsar through `shared/radial.js`). Drawn vector: the value cut into bands at ΔE 1, stacked dark → bright, outlines by `Organica.isoContours` (d3-contour's algorithm, ISC — the THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES line is the main session's). TuneSutra's Colour mode preview is drawn by the same `fieldValue`. **The names Wash, Slope, Ripple, Wash over, Each shape, All the shapes are provisional** — O-92; the model key `field` and its settings keys stay whatever the names become. | One maths for the picture Diego chose: the preview, the screen and every export draw the same fill | `shared/shade-editor.js` (`FIELD_RANGES`), `shared/shading.js` (`fieldValue`, `fieldPlates`), `shared/core.js` (`isoContours`, `simplifyRing`); `scripts/test-shading.mjs`, `scripts/test-shading-field.mjs`, `scripts/test-wash-tools.mjs`; `/design-system/#shade-editor` |
| 2026-11-21 | **TuneSutra palettes whole in every tool — one kit, nine colours, one RMX math, no built-ins** (Diego, Nov 20–21, 2026: *porta il nuovo palette su tutti gli altri tools*; his answers to the plan; provisional words O-91). (1) **Every tool takes 9 colours**, Camo Turing included (its shader `uPalette[9]`); a generate strip defaults to 9. (2) **Two kinds of tool, one module — `Organica.paletteKit`** (`shared/palette-kit.js`, FVS's Nov 20 wiring made generic): a **strip tool** (a list of colours — FVS, Trellis, Spore, Pollen, Mote, Camo Turing, Membrane, Vortex) takes a palette's name whole (`kit.strip`: colours, each colour's shading where the tool has filled shapes, the Colour mode, *Save as palette…*); a **role tool** (fixed colour rows) fills **every row by the house role** from any row's ▦ (`kit.roles`, `palette.swatch({ onPalette })`): Radial, Pulsar, Warping, Camo Turing / Mote / Pollen / Membrane's single rows — Ink ← *main* (or *sub*, else the darkest), Paper ← *background* (or *ground*, else the lightest); Halide + Background fill ← *ground · middle · background*; Living Path, Sinew — Ink · Paper (`bg`); Blob Boundary — Background ← *background*, Mask ← *main · sub*, Dots ← *accent · accent2 · sub*; Komorebi — Sun ← *accent · sub*, Sky ← *background*, Ground ← *ground · main*, Shadow ← the darkest; Vortex — Background ← *background · ground*. Colornet's Background ▦ sets the background by role and the channels by index (the background's colour left out; a notice when the palette has more colours than channels). A palette colour is used once while another is left. (3) **Shading in every tool with filled shapes** — FVS, Trellis, Spore (each mark in its own box), Radial (Fill mode), Halide (ink regions), Living Path / Sinew (letters), Blob Boundary (mask), Colornet (flat channels): `Organica.shading.overlaySVG` / `overlayCanvas` on the shape's box, clipped to it; the editor `Organica.shadeEditor` is mounted **between panel sections, never inside one**; saved in presets (an older preset opens flat). Plates count each shadow ink (`Organica.plateExport.inksOf` / `plateSVG`, promoted from FVS, index-based). (4) **Riso inks and the built-in combinations removed everywhere** — `palette.library()` = saved + starred only (amends the Nov 20 row: the built-ins no longer stay for Variations › Another palette, which is left out when the library is empty; the hero field keeps its own three only when there is no palette). (5) **The Colour mode is set in TuneSutra and saved with the palette** (`mapping: { mode, submode, order, pair, space }`, `Organica.palettes.mappingFor` / `rampOf`; defaults for older and starred palettes: Inks, Tone, by lightness, OKLab) — a strip tool takes the colours dark → bright in its order (`order: 'ramp'`). (6) **One RMX math, Pollen's** — `Organica.palette.colorAt` (Posterize rounds, Tone + random ±1.2 of a stop) in Camo Turing's shader and its JS twin, Membrane, Spore, Mote; Camo and Membrane exports change slightly at the same settings (**accepted**). (7) **Adaptive** everywhere (Mote's *Adapt* → *Adaptive*); Spore's **Image · Multi** and Mote's **Video** stay. | One palette made in TuneSutra looks and behaves the same in every tool: its colours, its shading and how it is laid on a tone travel with it; one list, one math, no second source of palettes | `shared/palette-kit.js`, `shared/palette.js` (`onPalette`, `library()`, max 9), `shared/palettes.js` (`mapping`), `shared/shading.js` (`boxOf`, `overlaySVG`, `overlayCanvas`), `shared/plate-export.js` (`inksOf`, `plateSVG`); `scripts/test-rmx.mjs`, `test-palette-colour-tools.mjs`, `test-palette-role-tools.mjs`, `test-palette-radial.mjs`, `test-palette-spore-halide.mjs`, `test-palettes.mjs` |

| 2026-11-20 | **TuneSutra's palettes reach every tool whole — one central module** (Diego: *fai un analisi per … portare le palette salvate in tunesutra in tutti i tool … caricarle con tutti i loro shadow, grain e parametri … primo tool è fvs ma deve funzionare come unico modulo centralizzato ovunque*; plan answers and CONSULT gaps G1/G2/G7/G8). (1) ~~**The light falls on each shape**~~ — *amended the same day (row above): one light across the whole Element by default, Light › Each layer to split it*: a shaded ink draws its Gradient + Shadow in each shape's own box, clipped to the shape (as on TuneSutra's house). (2) **The palette menu = TuneSutra's Saved, then Starred** (`.pi-group` labels *Saved · Starred*; empty: *No palettes yet — save or star one in TuneSutra*); the Riso inks and the three built-in combinations leave the menus (`palette.library()` keeps them for Variations › Another palette and the hero field); the FVS **other Elements' Palettes leave the menu** (G8). (3) **Saving from a tool always makes a NEW palette** — *Save as palette…* (`.mini-btn` under the strip) → `Organica.prompt` → *Palette saved as “‹name›”*; the copy is named **‹name› · Flexible Visual System** (the tool's visible name, G7), numbered when taken; the original never changes; a stable `pid` + `from`. | 2026-11-20 | **TuneSutra: Add a colour picks the new colour by the role rule** (Diego: *Yes, role rule*; replaces the Nov 18 *a new colour follows the harmony, Complementary by default*). | 2026-11-20 | **One shared icon button on content: `.org-btn--on-content`, a circle** (Diego: *Yes, one shared form · cerchio*; then *20 px · Pieno d’inchiostro* on hover · saved *Pieno d’inchiostro* · the halo rule *Sì, scrivila*). Five buttons did the same job five ways (TuneSutra card star and `.ts-remove`, FVS `.fvs-thumb-quicksave` / `.fvs-thumb-tile`, FVS `.fg-var__tools`). Method = O-34's: nearest step of each ink's OKLCH scale reaching 3:1 on `--paper` and `--panel` — light: rule → `#a27e00`, figure → `#c36800`, the rest as sampled; dark: canvas → `#838383`, the rest as sampled. Nearest pair ΔE 7.6 light (rule / figure), 9.0 dark (palette / composition). Overline on the tinted head ≥ 5.0:1, name ≥ 12:1. The palette is NOT added to the palette library — nodes only. ## 3. Standing exceptions

Things that look like violations and are not. The lint allow-lists
(`scripts/css-lint.py`: `ALLOW_SHADOW`, `ALLOW_RADIUS`, `ALLOW_HEX`, `KNOWN_DEBT`; `scripts/templates.py`:
`OWN_SURFACE`, `OWN_LAYOUT`, `ALLOW`) are the executable half of this list — an exception lives there **and** here, or it is not one.

| What | Where | Why it is allowed |
|---|---|---|
| FVS quick-save: hovering a saved ✓ fills it `--danger` (Nov 20, 2026) | `fvs/fvs.css` `.org-btn.fvs-thumb-quicksave.saved.delete-hover` | The one button on content whose hover previews a delete; the rest follow `.org-btn--on-content`’s ink hover |
| **Node palette under 3:1** (Diego, Oct 8, 2026) — `--port-rule` yellow `#fac400` (1.6:1 on white), `--port-figure` orange `#fb8700` (2.5:1) as port dots and wires; content green head text 4.47:1; the pill icons are always white (Diego: "the icons on the pills must be light") — and the round cap of a capped card (the Figure / Variation, orange: 2.5:1; Oct 9, 2026) — 1.6:1 on yellow, 2.5:1 on orange, ≥ 3.4:1 on pink / red / green, 4.5:1 on blue, 18.6:1 on black | `shared/tokens.css`, `shared/node-canvas.css`; the `/design-system/` self-check exempts the ports and checks head text ≥ 4.4:1 | Diego wants the cover's colours exactly. A port is never told by colour alone: label + shape (square = list), the dot filled with a darker edge. |
| The quick-save circle's "remove" is `close`, not `trash` | FVS `quickSaveButton` (Component gallery, Element views / frame) | O-17, Diego Oct 3, 2026: the circle toggles a thumbnail's saved state in place (saved → unsaved; the view stays), so `close` reads as "undo the save"; `trash` would read as "destroy this". |
| Content colours typed as hex | every tool's ink / paper / point / grid colour (e.g. Loom's guide blue, Halide's ink and paper) | User data, not chrome (`CLAUDE.md` Critical Rules) |
| Stadium radii off the 2/4/8 scale | switch track, scrollbar thumbs, Camo Turing's toggle | Half the element's height is a capsule, not a corner |
| Canvas text and colour drawn in the artwork | Loom cell labels, TuneSutra bar labels | Output, not UI (`docs/CSS-RULES.md` (c)) |
| Genesis's local panel implementation and skeleton | `genesis/index.html`; `ALLOW` in `scripts/templates.py` (rules `app`, `floatbar-child`, `panel-last`, `tail-scripts`) | Retiring — left alone by decision (Sep 2026, confirmed Oct 2, 2026) |
| The floatbar pill animates `width` | `shared/floatbar.css` | Measured 60 fps (233 frames, max 17.7 ms); `scaleX` would squash its ends |
| Raw `1ms` durations | reduced-motion blocks | The collapse value itself |
| TuneSutra's share-tube **pour**: a JS damped spring `1 − e^(−4.2s)·cos(7.5s)` on a 1000 ms time base (`POUR_MS`, settle `SETTLE_MS` 1200) — literal ms, no `--ease-*` (Diego, Nov 18, 2026: *Yes, record it*) | `tunesutra/index.html` `pour()` | An oscillating curve (overshoot and settle) cannot be a cubic-bezier token; the stagger reads `--dur-stagger`, it runs only when a palette opens or the colour count changes, never under `prefers-reduced-motion` and never in an export |
| `.hud-btn` raw hex `#f5f2ec` | `shared/shell.css` | A dark HUD pill over the canvas, identical in both themes |
| `explorations/*` and the `_`-prefixed dev pages | `explorations/`, `design-system/_*.html`, `*/_test-*.html` | Development pages; not held to the tool contract, skipped by `scripts/templates.py` (`SKIP`). The test gallery is on the Page template since Oct 2, 2026 — a development page in content, no longer an exception in skeleton |
| Own-surface tools — tool chrome (header, floatbar, `#panel`, mobile gate) without `shell.css` | Apostate, Flexible Visual System, Rhizome, Genesis — `OWN_SURFACE` in `scripts/templates.py`, each with its reason | A variant of the Tool template, not a fourth one (O-6). The lint still holds them to every Tool rule except linking `shell.css` |
| Apostate's three-column board | `apostate/index.html` (character picker · glyph · panel) | typoclast's own layout, kept by the "start from zero" decision; recorded as an exception under O-9 either way |
| Own-layout pages — page chrome without the `page.css` column | the hub (`index.html`: stipple hero, gallery, full-bleed sections) and `/design-system/` (a 232px section nav beside the content) — `OWN_LAYOUT` in `scripts/templates.py` | Variants of the Page template (O-6) |
| Rhizome loads every shared script in `<head>` | `rhizome/index.html`; `ALLOW[('rhizome', 'tail-scripts')]` | Native ES modules: the shared scripts must exist before the module graph runs |
| Pages without a mobile gate | privacy, terms, admin, sign-in, design-system (the gallery and the hub opt in) | A phone visitor must be able to read a policy or sign in (`docs/CSS-RULES.md` (a)); the gate is required by the Tool template only, opt-in on a page |

| 2026-10-09 | **From the REVIEW of rules-as-a-chain** (notes): (a) **the migration keeps wire order; the old engine did not apply wire order** — before, a Component rule always posed first and Cell rules applied after it, the *first* Component rule won (`rules.find`), and Rotate & mirror applied after the last Repeat whatever its wire. `chainRules()` chains in wire order, so a saved graph with Cell rules wired before a Component rule now loses their turns, one with two Component rules switches pose, and one whose Rotate & mirror was wired before its Repeat in grid now shows an error. The `migrated` test compares against the new chain, not the pre-change drawing. Fix: order a migrated chain as the old engine applied it — first Component rule, then the Cell rules, then the Repeats in wire order, then the last Rotate & mirror — and check it against a hash recorded before the change. (b) copy: the Component rule refusal prints the lattice id in lower case (*a triangle lattice*, *a hexagon lattice*) where the Grid panel says *Triangle lattice* / *Hexagon lattice* (UI-COPY §1, on-screen name not id) — build it from the generator label. (c) the migration is silent while `safeModel()` says what it repaired — consider a notice when a graph was rewired. (d) a copied rule node lands 32 px under its original and may overlap cards. | `fvs/js/17-figure-graph.js` `chainRules` (~1482); `fvs/js/engine/17-figure-nodes.js` Component rule refusal (~289) |

Propose a shared reorder helper (`shared/core.js` or `panel`-side JS) when a third tool needs list reorder, or when Living Path moves to the pointer drag — whichever comes first (`docs/CSS-RULES.md` (d), JS module = promote at the second consumer of the *same* code). *Nov 21, 2026: the third instance (TuneSutra's Order) promoted `Organica.reorder` (§2); FVS layers and Living Path still to move onto it.* | `fvs/index.html:6059-6095`, `livingpath/index.html:821` |
